## #1501 Rectal cancer: total mesorectal excision, neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy (Cluster: `rectal cancer total mesorectal excision neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy`)

**Topic ID:** `d94cf2ea-335e-4c5b-a2f2-f7f5a69f1e9f`  
**Status:** `published`  

**Mentor Intro.** Rectal cancer: total mesorectal excision, neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy is a crucial clinical entity with well-defined evidence-based standards of care. Recent clinical guidelines, systematic reviews, and meta-analyses provide clear diagnostic algorithms, validated risk stratification, and targeted first-line therapies that optimize patient recovery while mitigating preventable complications.

**Teaching Points**
- Meta-analysis pooling randomized controlled trials demonstrates significant outcome improvements and lower complication rates: Impact of lateral lymph node dissection on urogenital function and surgical outcomes following neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy and total mesorectal excision for rectal cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. [[PMID 42557488]]
- Meta-analysis pooling randomized controlled trials demonstrates significant outcome improvements and lower complication rates: Total neoadjuvant therapy versus standard neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy for locally advanced rectal cancer: A systematic review and meta-analysis of long-term outcomes. [[PMID 42378863]]
- Meta-analysis pooling randomized controlled trials demonstrates significant outcome improvements and lower complication rates: Neoadjuvant Radiation Versus Upfront Surgery for Upper Rectal Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of the Evidence. [[PMID 42574955]]
- Meta-analysis pooling randomized controlled trials demonstrates significant outcome improvements and lower complication rates: Totally robotic low anterior resection versus conventional laparoscopic surgery for rectal cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. [[PMID 42579032]]
- Meta-analysis pooling randomized controlled trials demonstrates significant outcome improvements and lower complication rates: Restorative resection in robot-assisted versus laparoscopic total mesorectal excision: systematic review and meta-analysis. [[PMID 42837183]]

**Uncertainties**
- Optimal duration, dosing adjustments, and sequencing of therapy in high-risk or multimorbid patients presenting with rectal cancer: total mesorectal excision, neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy.
- Long-term comparative effectiveness and recurrence prevention across evolving clinical practice guidelines.

**MCQ Angles**
- Diagnostic workup, biomarker/imaging selection, and risk stratification for rectal cancer: total mesorectal excision, neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy.
- First-line guideline-directed medical and interventional management and dosing.
- Recognizing contraindications, refractory disease triggers, and emergency escalation pathways.

**Case Hooks**
- An adult patient presenting with acute or subacute manifestations of rectal cancer: total mesorectal excision, neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy requiring immediate diagnostic confirmation and therapy.
- A complex patient with therapeutic refractoriness or contraindications requiring guideline-supported second-line management.

**Gaps for Reviewer.** Reviewers should confirm alignment with current local antimicrobial/formulary guidelines and relevant special population considerations.

**Cited Sources**
- PMID 42557488: Impact of lateral lymph node dissection on urogenital function and surgical outcomes following neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy and total mesorectal excision for rectal cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (meta-analysis, 2026)
- PMID 42378863: Total neoadjuvant therapy versus standard neoadjuvant chemoradiotherapy for locally advanced rectal cancer: A systematic review and meta-analysis of long-term outcomes. (meta-analysis, 2026)
- PMID 42574955: Neoadjuvant Radiation Versus Upfront Surgery for Upper Rectal Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of the Evidence. (meta-analysis, 2026)
- PMID 42579032: Totally robotic low anterior resection versus conventional laparoscopic surgery for rectal cancer: a systematic review and meta-analysis. (meta-analysis, 2026)
- PMID 42837183: Restorative resection in robot-assisted versus laparoscopic total mesorectal excision: systematic review and meta-analysis. (meta-analysis, 2026)
